Round 4 的错误落在哪一步？

留出测试集 · 169 段故事 · 700 道题 × 4 个种子 · 下图按作答次数计

2,800 次作答　2,141 对 · 659 错　｜　准确率 76.46%
按实际发送给模型的故事分流　↓
回退到 PICTURE　1,175
729 对 · 446 错
占全部错误的 67.7%
Harness 过滤了对话　1,625
1,412 对 · 213 错
占全部错误的 32.3%

沿过滤分支往下看

1,625 次过滤260 次缺少可对齐标注，错 29 次
1,365 次可核验128 次有多给或漏给，错 27 次
1,237 次原句逐句吻合285 次未纳入严格单人核验，错 36 次
952 次一阶题，人物和原句均正确831 对 · 121 错

这 121 次，最终答案具体错在哪里？

57把不知情判成知情
46把知情判成不知情
14两种具体说法选错
4答错所指人物

121 次涉及 43 道不同题。按标答与实际所选选项的含义归类；宽度仅表示核验层级。

哪里还有提升空间？

回退路径错 446 次。其中 289 次所选人物全程在场，简单延长缺席词表帮不到这批；71 次确有离开后返回，3 次中途加入，5 次离开未回，另有 78 次无对齐标注。同一批回退作答，PICTURE 得 728 分，Round 4 得 729 分。

对 128 次筛句有差异的作答，保持选人、模板和种子，再分别用原窗口和标注窗口重跑后两步。原窗口答对 101 次，标注窗口答对 97 次。回退与有差异的窗口各有多少错误，只表示各组的改进空间；真实收益须用同题对照测量。

在 952 次严格核验作答上，PICTURE 对 508 次，Round 4 对 831 次。仅修好其中 121 个错答且保住其余答案，全测试可增加 4.32 个百分点。

两条真实记录

第 29 题，种子 333。Silas 到场后只听说大家在聊育儿。模型先写“小组对亲子情感联系的立场没有传达”，随即从后来友善的交谈推断他知道小组的具体立场，最后选错。

第 1348 题，种子 111。Kristopher 来晚了，但 Miranda 当着他的面说 Brian 在优胜美地遇到熊。模型引用了这句原话，仍以“他缺席了事情经过，不知道细节”为由选“不知道是哪种动物”。题目只问动物种类。

第 25 题 · 把不知情判成知情 · 错 4 次
种子 0 · 标答 (a)第一次选人
Silas
第二次请求 · 用户消息
Answer this belief question using only the dialogue available to Silas. The gap hides speech that this person did not hear. Do not reconstruct it from an option, a related topic, or general knowledge. Statements after arrival or return are visible: retain specific information actually stated, but a broad summary does not reveal unstated details. A person can know their own statements and those heard while present. For nested beliefs, distinguish outer believer Silas from inner believer Silas; shared conversation and observable statements can support what the outer believer thinks the inner person knows.

Dialogue:
[Conversation occurred while Silas was absent; its specific contents are unavailable to Silas.]
Silas: Hello everyone, sorry for jumping in late. What's the topic?
Kobe: No worries, Silas. We were just sharing our experiences about parenting.
Silas: Oh, that sounds interesting! In that case, can I start by sharing a memorable story about my son?
Elena: Of course, Silas! We'd love to hear it.
Silas: Okay, brace yourselves. When my son was about four, we went to a zoo. He was so fascinated by all the animals and spent hours just watching them. When we were about to leave, he looked up at me with the most serious expression and asked, “Dad, can we bring a lion home?”
Everyone: (Laughs)
Richard: That's adorable, Silas! Children's innocent questions and observations can be so entertaining!
Silas: True, Richard. It was a moment I'll never forget. He was so disappointed when I had to explain why having a lion at home was not practical.
Elena: That’s cute and hilarious! And you're right, these are the moments that make all the struggles and challenges worth it.
Kobe: I couldn’t have put it better myself, Elena. Silas, do keep these stories coming. They make the parenting journey seem even more enriching.
Silas: Will do, Kobe. And I have plenty where that one came from!
Richard: I'm sure you do, Silas. These experiential insights are indeed precious. They remind us of the lighter side of parenting and the joy that comes along with it.
Elena: Totally agree with you, Richard. Looking forward to our next chat session already!
Kobe: Definitely, this was a fun and enlightening discussion. Can't wait for the next one!

Question: What does Silas believe about the pace and style of individual child development discussed by the group?
(a) Silas is unaware about the pace and style of individual child development discussed by the group because he was not involved in the conversation when this was discussed.
(b) Silas believes that every child has its own pace and style of learning, with no definitive timetable for developmental milestones, and the range of what is considered 'normal' is quite wide. He agrees with the group's emphasis on the uniqueness of each child and the importance of not comparing a child's development with others. Furthermore, he thinks that focusing on enjoying each moment instead of falling into the comparison trap is crucial.

In at most three short bullets, cite the most relevant visible statements, identify any specific claims not conveyed, and say which option is better supported. Do not invent dialogue.
第二次模型原答
Relevant visible statements:
- The group was sharing their experiences about parenting.
- Elena: "These are the moments that make all the struggles and challenges worth it."
- Kobe: "The parenting journey seems even more enriching."
- Richard: "These experiential insights are indeed precious. They remind us of the lighter side of parenting and the joy that comes along with it."

Specific claims not conveyed:
- The specific pace and style of individual child development discussed by the group when Silas was absent.
- The exact nature of the "struggles and challenges" Elena mentioned.

Option (b) is better supported because:
- Silas agrees with the group's emphasis on the uniqueness of each child (Elena: "These are the moments that make all the struggles and challenges worth it.").
- Silas shares a story that highlights the importance of enjoying each moment with his child, rather than focusing on developmental milestones (Silas: "He was so disappointed when I had to explain why having a lion at home was not practical").
- Silas's tone and the group's responses suggest that they value the individuality and experiences of each child, rather than comparing them to others.
第三次模型原答
(b)

Question: What does Silas believe were the responsibilities as parents mentioned and discussed by the group?
(a) Silas believes that the responsibilities as parents discussed by the group were fostering emotional connections with their children, creating a safe space for open communication, allowing children to learn on their own yet providing necessary protection and nurturing, and refraining from comparing their children's development with others'. He also understood that balancing career responsibilities with being there for their children and dealing with parenting challenges were a part of parental duties.
(b) Silas is unaware of the discussed responsibilities as parents because he was not involved in the conversation when this topic was discussed.

In at most three short bullets, cite the most relevant visible statements, identify any specific claims not conveyed, and say which option is better supported. Do not invent dialogue.

- The specific responsibilities as parents discussed by the group when Silas was absent.
- The exact nature of the "struggles and challenges" mentioned by Elena.

Option (b) is better supported because the visible statements do not provide any information about the specific responsibilities as parents discussed by the group when Silas was absent.

Question: What does Silas believe about the group's stance on fostering strong emotional connections with their children?
(a) Silas does not know about the group's stance on fostering strong emotional connections with their children because he was not involved in the conversation when this was discussed.
(b) Silas believes that the group's stance on fostering strong emotional connections with their children is of great importance. He supports the ideas of creating a bond through open communication and making a safe space for emotional growth. He also agrees with the involvement of considerations regarding the child's emotional well-being in conjunction with their physical health.

In at most three short bullets, cite the most relevant visible statements, identify any specific claims not conveyed, and say which option is better supported. Do not invent dialogue.
第二次模型原答
Relevant visible statements:
- Elena: "That’s cute and hilarious! And you're right, these are the moments that make all the struggles and challenges worth it."
- Kobe: "I couldn’t have put it better myself, Elena. Silas, do keep these stories coming. They make the parenting journey seem even more enriching."
- Richard: "It was a moment I'll never forget. He was so disappointed when I had to explain why having a lion at home was not practical."
- Elena: "Totally agree with you, Richard. Looking forward to our next chat session already!"

Specific claims not conveyed:
- The group's stance on fostering strong emotional connections with their children before Silas joined the conversation.
- The specific content of the conversation Silas missed.

Option (b) is better supported because:
- The group's positive reactions to Silas's story and their appreciation for his sharing experiences suggest that they value emotional connections with their children.
- The tone of the conversation is supportive and encouraging, indicating that they prioritize the emotional well-being of their children.

Aden: Wholeheartedly agree, Beau. We're all exploring and learning about our connection to this cosmic fabric in our individual ways and pace.

Robin: That's so true, Jose. In Italy, too, the saying 'La famiglia mangia insieme' means 'Family eats together'. It's not just about the food but also about sharing it with your loved ones, which has deep cultural roots.
Sage: Yeah, Robin. That's the essence of enjoying food. It's so interesting how it not just satiates our hunger but also serves as a lens to explore, understand and appreciate diverse cultures.

Sage: Indeed Bryson! The joy of experiencing new foods is the joy of connecting with different cultures on a very intimate level. It allows a peek into one's way of life, and that's incredible!
Jose: I guess, at the end of the day, that's the beauty of cultural exchange. Like in food, it's all about mixing and trying new things. Finding similarities in differences. And that's what makes it so enriching.
Robin: Well said everyone. Whether it's Tacos, BBQ, Pasta or Sushi, it's all about appreciating and enjoying the different flavors of the world. Isn't it wonderful that we are so different, yet so very similar!

Dialogue:
[Conversation occurred while Theodore was absent; its specific contents are unavailable to Theodore.]
Theodore: Hello ladies! I hope I'm not interrupting anything major here?
Kristin: Not at all, Theodore! We were just discussing our personal experiences with music and its significant impact on us.
Theodore: That sounds like an interesting conversation. As we're discussing personal experiences, has there been a time when a controversy or scandal related to music has affected you?
Tabitha: That's an interesting question, Theodore. I remember when the scandal surrounding Milli Vanilli broke out. It was a massive shock, and it kind of shattered my trust in the music industry for a while.
Kristin: I can relate to that, Tabitha. For me, it was the controversy surrounding the use of autotune in music. It made me question the authenticity of many songs.
Theodore: That's an excellent point, Kristin. Technology has always been a double-edged sword for the music industry. On the one hand, it has allowed for innovation and growth. On the other hand, it has opened up the floodgates for controversies and scandals.
Tabitha: You're correct, Theodore. It all boils down to integrity and authenticity in the end. Along with the joy and creativity music brings, it is also essential for musicians to uphold certain ethical standards, which in turn impacts the trust of their listeners.
Kristin: I completely agree with both of you. Incidents like these are reminders that while music serves as a cultural and emotional medium, it is also tied with industry standards and expectations which ought to be respected.
Theodore: Unequivocally true. And while we might have experienced disappointments, music remains this incredible unifying force that transcends any controversy or scandal. After all, we're still here, loving and enjoying music in all its forms.
Kristin: Absolutely, Theodore. For every disappointment, there's a moment of shared joy in discovering a new song, a new artist, or simply in hearing a beloved track. That's the real power of music.
Tabitha: Undeniably, music plugs us into a river of emotions, making us feel deeply connected with one another, regardless of such setbacks. It's incredible that despite the scandals and controversies we mentioned, the power of music is such that it still manages to shine through and maintain its impact, don't you think?
Theodore: Yes, Tabitha. That truly speaks to the strength and resilience of music as an art, doesn't it?

Question: What does Theodore believe about how music can contribute to the local and global economy?
(a) Theodore believes that music significantly contributes to both the local and global economy in numerous ways. He believes that sales from recorded music, concert tickets, merchandise, and licensing fees contribute to an economy at a local level. Additionally, Theodore thinks that music festivals and live performances can boost local businesses and tourism. On a global scale, Theodore believes that music contributes to the economy by breaking down borders and cultural barriers, creating international markets and revenue through digital streaming, record sales and international tours. He therefore sees the music industry as having a significant impact on job creation and economic output at both local and global levels.
(b) Theodore is unaware of how music can contribute to the local and global economy because he was not involved in the conversation when this topic was discussed.

In at most three short bullets, cite the most relevant visible statements, identify any specific claims not conveyed, and say which option is better supported. Do not invent dialogue.
第二次模型原答
Relevant visible statements:
- Theodore mentions that technology has been a "double-edged sword" for the music industry, implying a mix of benefits and drawbacks.
- Tabitha and Kristin discuss the importance of integrity and authenticity in the music industry, which is tied to industry standards and expectations.
- Theodore and the others agree that music remains a unifying force despite controversies and scandals.

Specific claims not conveyed:
- There is no discussion about the economic impact of the music industry.
- The conversation focuses on the emotional and cultural significance of music, rather than its economic effects.

Option (b) is better supported because the conversation does not mention the economic impact of the music industry, and Theodore does not express any thoughts on this topic.
